country_event = {
	id = 2000000
	title = "EVTNAME2000000"
	desc = "EVTDESC2000000"
	picture = "Administration"
	trigger = {
		accepted_culture = north_german
		NOT = {
			any_owned_province = {
				culture = north_german
				OR = {
					is_colonial = no
					owner = {
						citizenship_policy = full_citizenship
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 60
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.1
			citizenship_policy = limited_citizenship
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.01
			citizenship_policy = residency
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA2000000"
		remove_accepted_culture = north_german
	}
}
province_event = {
	id = 2000001
	title = "EVTNAME2000001"
	desc = "EVTDESC2000001"
	trigger = {
		is_primary_culture = no
		is_accepted_culture = yes
		culture = north_german
		total_pops = 5000
		average_militancy = 4
		owner = {
			OR = {
				capital_scope = {
					NOT = {
						culture = north_german
					}
				}
				citizenship_policy = residency
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 12
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.1
			average_militancy = 6
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.1
			average_militancy = 8
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 10
			owner = {
				citizenship_policy = limited_citizenship
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 100
			owner = {
				citizenship_policy = full_citizenship
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA2000001"
		owner = {
			remove_accepted_culture = north_german
		}
	}
}
province_event = {
	id = 2000002
	title = "EVTNAME2000002"
	desc = "EVTDESC2000002"
	trigger = {
		is_accepted_culture = no
		culture = north_german
		total_pops = 40000
		NOT = {
			average_militancy = 0.4
		}
		OR = {
			is_colonial = no
			owner = {
				citizenship_policy = full_citizenship
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 240
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			NOT = {
				average_militancy = 0.2
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			NOT = {
				average_militancy = 0.1
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 5
			owner = {
				citizenship_policy = limited_citizenship
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 25
			owner = {
				citizenship_policy = residency
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 14
			is_colonial = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA2000002"
		owner = {
			add_accepted_culture = north_german
		}
	}
}

		is_accepted_culture = yes
		culture = portuguese
		total_pops = 5000
		average_militancy = 4
		owner = {
			OR = {
				capital_scope = {
					NOT = {
						culture = portuguese
					}
				}
				citizenship_policy = residency
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 12
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.1
			average_militancy = 6
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.1
			average_militancy = 8
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 10
			owner = {
				citizenship_policy = limited_citizenship
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 100
			owner = {
				citizenship_policy = full_citizenship
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA2000064"

		}

	id = 2000896
	title = "EVTNAME2000896"
	desc = "EVTDESC2000896"
	trigger = {
		is_accepted_culture = no
		culture = beja
		total_pops = 40000
		NOT = {
			average_militancy = 0.4
		}
		OR = {
			is_colonial = no
			owner = {
				citizenship_policy = full_citizenship
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 240
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			NOT = {
				average_militancy = 0.2
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			NOT = {
				average_militancy = 0.1
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 5
			owner = {
				citizenship_policy = limited_citizenship
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 25
			owner = {
				citizenship_policy = residency
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 14
			is_colonial = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA2000896"
		owner = {
			add_accepted_culture = beja
		}
	}
}

	desc = "EVTDESC2000981"
	picture = "Administration"
	trigger = {
		accepted_culture = kalenjin
		NOT = {
			any_owned_province = {
				culture = kalenjin
				OR = {
					is_colonial = no
					owner = {
						citizenship_policy = full_citizenship
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 60
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.1
			citizenship_policy = limited_citizenship
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.01
			citizenship_policy = residency
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA2000981"
		remove_accepted_culture = kalenjin
	}
}

	desc = "EVTDESC2001045"
	trigger = {
		is_primary_culture = no
		is_accepted_culture = yes
		culture = xhosa
		total_pops = 5000
		average_militancy = 4
		owner = {
			OR = {
				capital_scope = {
					NOT = {
						culture = xhosa
					}
				}
				citizenship_policy = residency
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 12
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.1
			average_militancy = 6
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.1
			average_militancy = 8
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 10
			owner = {
				citizenship_policy = limited_citizenship
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 100
			owner = {
				citizenship_policy = full_citizenship
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA2001045"
		owner = {

		}
	}
